attribute kinds — 2026-11-10

kind 1 = "NN_a of X" (numbered): X has it, EVERY specific inherits it.
kind 2 = an attribute only SOME specifics have: it belongs under that specific "X.s'a" / its specific-division, not numbered as X's.
Candidates for kind 2 = UNNUMBERED attribute sections "a of X" of a concept X WITH specifics (X's specific-tree:: links, or X.s children),
  not fixed (info-resource, evoluting, DOING, structure, evaluation, overview, MISC-ATTRIBUTE, naming, relation, Article, trees).
Your decision per candidate: number it (kind 1, all specifics have it) or move it under the specific(s) / division that have it (kind 2).
Hint "k with own a": how many of X's specifics have their own section with the same attribute word.
